//------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Type Definitions
//------------------------------------------------------------------------------

// "AFX_CONNECTIONMAP"
struct AFX_CONNECTIONMAP
{
	struct AFX_CONNECTIONMAP const* (__stdcall* pfnGetBaseMap)();
	void const* pEntry;
};

// "AFX_DISPMAP"
struct AFX_DISPMAP
{
	struct AFX_DISPMAP const* (__stdcall* pfnGetBaseMap)();
	void const* lpEntries;
	uint32_t* lpEntryCount;
	uint32_t* lpStockPropMask;
};

// "AFX_EVENTSINKMAP"
struct AFX_EVENTSINKMAP
{
	struct AFX_EVENTSINKMAP const* (__stdcall* pfnGetBaseMap)();
	void const* lpEntries;
	uint32_t* lpEntryCount;
};

// "AFX_INTERFACEMAP"
struct AFX_INTERFACEMAP
{
	struct AFX_INTERFACEMAP const* (__stdcall* pfnGetBaseMap)();
	void const* pEntry;
};

// "AFX_OLECMDMAP"
struct AFX_OLECMDMAP
{
	struct AFX_OLECMDMAP const* (__stdcall* pfnGetBaseMap)();
	void const* lpEntries;
};

// "AINet"
struct AINet
{
	int32_t netId;
	char name[0x14];
	enum AINetType aiType;
	float pathWidth;
	float activateRadius;
	float attackRadius;
	float attackDwell;
	float notPursuitDwell;
	float pursuitParam0;
	float pursuitParam1;
	float returnRange;
	float hideTime0;
	float hideTime1;
	int32_t activateBuddyNetId;
	int32_t attackBuddyNetId;
	enum AINetAttackStrategy attackStrategy;
	struct AINetNode* nodeListHead;
	struct AINet* next;
};

// "AINetAttackStrategy"
enum AINetAttackStrategy : uint32_t
{
	AINET_STRAT_HEA = 0x0,
	AINET_STRAT_CIR = 0x1,
	AINET_STRAT_BAC = 0x2,
	AINET_STRAT_FOL = 0x3,

	int32_t rgbBitsTotalMinus8;
	int32_t gbBitsTotalMinus8;
	int32_t bShiftTo8;
	int32_t rgbBitsTotal;
	uint32_t rMaskShifted;
	uint32_t gMaskShifted;
	uint32_t bMaskShifted;
	uint32_t nonRgbMaskShifted;
};

// "zVideo_TextureRecord"

{
	struct IDirectDrawSurface* m_uploadSurface;
	struct IDirectDrawSurface* m_textureSurface;
	struct IDirect3DTexture2* m_texture;
	int32_t m_textureHandle;
	int32_t m_alphaMode;
	int32_t m_uWrapMode;
	int32_t m_vWrapMode;
};

typedef int32_t (__fastcall* zVideo_UnlockTextureUploadCallback)(struct zVideo_TextureRecord* textureRecord);

// "zWorldArea"

	float cellMinZ;

// "zZarFileRecord"

// "zZbdDataReadyCallback"
typedef void (__fastcall* zZbdDataReadyCallback)(struct zZbdDataReadyCtx* ctx, char* sectionToken, void* buffer, uint32_t size, void* userData);

// "zZbdDataReadyCtx"
struct zZbdDataReadyCtx
{
	struct zZbdManager* manager;
	struct zZbdSectionHandler* sectionHandler;
};

// "zZbdManager"
struct zZbdManager
{
	uint8_t sectionHandlerListAllocator;
	uint8_t reserved01[0x3];
	struct zZbdSectionHandlerNode* sectionHandlerListSentinel;
	int32_t sectionHandlerCount;
	struct zIndexArchive archive;
	uint32_t tempBufferSize;
	char* tempBuffer;
	int32_t unknown_2C;
	int32_t stopRequested;
};

// "zZbdPreLoadCallback"
typedef int32_t (__fastcall* zZbdPreLoadCallback)(struct zZbdPreLoadCtx* ctx, void* userData);

// "zZbdPreLoadCtx"
struct zZbdPreLoadCtx
{
	struct zZbdManager* manager;
	struct zZbdSectionHandler* sectionHandler;
};

// "zZbdSectionHandler"
struct zZbdSectionHandler
{
	char* sectionName;
	zZbdPreLoadCallback onPreLoad;
	zZbdDataReadyCallback onDataReady;
	int32_t sortOrder;
	void* userData;
};

// "zZbdSectionHandlerList"
struct zZbdSectionHandlerList
{
	uint8_t allocatorState;
	uint8_t reserved01[0x3];
	struct zZbdSectionHandlerNode* sentinel;
	int32_t count;
};

// "zZbdSectionHandlerNode"
struct zZbdSectionHandlerNode
{
	struct zZbdSectionHandlerNode* next;
	struct zZbdSectionHandlerNode* prev;
	struct zZbdSectionHandler sectionHandler;
};
